<script id="progressionAdvancementV2Script">
(()=>{'use strict';
const SYS={"key":"coc","title":"Call of Cthulhu Development Calculator"};
const DND_THRESHOLDS=[0,300,900,2700,6500,14000,23000,34000,48000,64000,85000,100000,120000,140000,165000,195000,225000,265000,305000,355000];
const PF_ADVERSARY={'-4':10,'-3':15,'-2':20,'-1':30,'0':40,'1':60,'2':80,'3':120,'4':160};
const PF_SIMPLE={'-4':2,'-3':3,'-2':4,'-1':6,'0':8,'1':12,'2':16,'3':24,'4':32};
const PF_COMPLEX=PF_ADVERSARY;
const byId=id=>document.getElementById(id), esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const num=(v,d=0)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):d, clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
const clone=v=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));
function S(){try{return state}catch(_){return window.__SHEET_TEST_API__?.getState?.()||{}}}
function ensure(){const s=S();s.progressionEngineV2=s.progressionEngineV2||{version:2,history:[],choices:[],settings:{pfThreshold:1000,daggerSessions:3,pbtaThreshold:5,gurpsSpent:0},pending:[],lastTrack:'playbook'};const e=s.progressionEngineV2;e.history=Array.isArray(e.history)?e.history:[];e.choices=Array.isArray(e.choices)?e.choices:[];e.settings=e.settings||{};if(!e.settings.pfThreshold)e.settings.pfThreshold=1000;if(!e.settings.daggerSessions)e.settings.daggerSessions=3;if(!e.settings.pbtaThreshold)e.settings.pbtaThreshold=5;return e}
function save(){try{if(typeof scheduleSave==='function')scheduleSave();else if(typeof STORE_KEY!=='undefined')localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY,JSON.stringify(S()))}catch(_){};try{if(typeof renderAll==='function')renderAll();else if(typeof window.render==='function')window.render()}catch(_){};setTimeout(render,0)}
function totalDndLevel(){const s=S();return Math.max(1,(s.classes||[]).reduce((a,c)=>a+num(c.level),0))}
function levelForXp(xp){let l=1;for(let i=1;i<DND_THRESHOLDS.length;i++)if(xp>=DND_THRESHOLDS[i])l=i+1;return l}
function swadeRank(n){return n>=16?'Legendary':n>=12?'Heroic':n>=8?'Veteran':n>=4?'Seasoned':'Novice'}
function currentBladesTrack(){const e=ensure(),v=e.lastTrack||'playbook';return ['playbook','insight','prowess','resolve','crew'].includes(v)?v:'playbook'}
function bladesValue(t){const s=S();return t==='crew'?num(s.crew?.crewXp):num(s.xp?.[t])}
function bladesThreshold(t){return t==='crew'?10:t==='playbook'?8:6}
function summary(){const s=S(),e=ensure();
 if(SYS.key==='dnd'){const xp=Math.max(0,num(s.profile?.xp)),char=totalDndLevel(),earned=levelForXp(xp),next=char>=20?null:DND_THRESHOLDS[char],prev=DND_THRESHOLDS[Math.max(0,char-1)]||0,span=next==null?1:Math.max(1,next-prev),pct=next==null?100:clamp((xp-prev)/span*100,0,100);return{primary:xp.toLocaleString()+' XP',secondary:'Character level '+char,next:next==null?'Maximum level':Math.max(0,next-xp).toLocaleString()+' XP to level '+(char+1),status:earned>char?'Level-up ready':'Tracking',pct,ready:earned>char,detail:'XP supports level '+earned+'.'}}
 if(SYS.key==='pathfinder'){const xp=Math.max(0,num(e.xp)),level=Math.max(1,num(s.profile?.level,1)),th=Math.max(1,num(e.settings.pfThreshold,1000));return{primary:xp+' / '+th+' XP',secondary:'Level '+level,next:Math.max(0,th-xp)+' XP remaining',status:xp>=th&&level<20?'Level-up ready':level>=20?'Maximum level':'Tracking',pct:level>=20?100:clamp(xp/th*100,0,100),ready:xp>=th&&level<20,detail:'Excess XP carries after level-up.'}}
 if(SYS.key==='fate'){const m=e.milestones||{minor:0,significant:0,major:0};return{primary:(num(m.minor)+num(m.significant)+num(m.major))+' milestones',secondary:`${num(m.minor)} minor · ${num(m.significant)} significant · ${num(m.major)} major`,next:'Milestones are story events',status:'Narrative advancement',pct:0,ready:false,detail:'Each award opens the matching change prompt.'}}
 if(SYS.key==='gurps'){const earned=num(e.gurpsEarned),spent=num(e.settings.gurpsSpent),available=Math.max(0,earned-spent);return{primary:available+' unspent CP',secondary:earned+' earned · '+spent+' spent',next:'GM-awarded character points',status:available>0?'Points available':'Tracking',pct:earned?clamp(available/earned*100,0,100):0,ready:available>0,detail:'No universal level threshold; spend only with GM approval.'}}
 if(SYS.key==='coc'){const checked=(s.skills||[]).filter(x=>x.checked&&!/cthulhu mythos/i.test(x.name||'')).length;return{primary:checked+' checked skills',secondary:'Development phase',next:checked?'Resolve improvement checks':'Mark skills after successful use',status:checked?'Checks ready':'Tracking',pct:checked?100:0,ready:checked>0,detail:'Each eligible check rolls separately.'}}
 if(SYS.key==='daggerheart'){const level=Math.max(1,num(s.profile?.level,1)),cur=num(e.daggerSessions),tar=Math.max(1,num(e.settings.daggerSessions,3));return{primary:'Level '+level,secondary:cur+' / '+tar+' milestone sessions',next:level>=10?'Maximum level':Math.max(0,tar-cur)+' session(s) to suggested review',status:level>=10?'Maximum level':cur>=tar?'Milestone ready':'Narrative tracking',pct:level>=10?100:clamp(cur/tar*100,0,100),ready:cur>=tar&&level<10,detail:'The table declares the narrative milestone; session count is an editable reminder.'}}
 if(SYS.key==='pbta'){const cur=Math.max(0,num(s.xp?.current)),max=Math.max(1,num(s.xp?.max||e.settings.pbtaThreshold,5));return{primary:cur+' / '+max+' XP',secondary:s.profile?.playbook||'Exact playbook',next:Math.max(0,max-cur)+' marks remaining',status:cur>=max?'Improvement ready':'Tracking',pct:clamp(cur/max*100,0,100),ready:cur>=max,detail:'Threshold and options remain specific to the selected PbtA game/playbook.'}}
 if(SYS.key==='swade'){const n=Math.max(num(e.swadeAdvances),(s.advances||[]).length),rank=swadeRank(n),next=n<4?4:n<8?8:n<12?12:n<16?16:null;return{primary:n+' Advances',secondary:rank,next:next?`${next-n} Advance(s) to ${swadeRank(next)}`:'Legendary progression',status:'Advance-based',pct:next?clamp((n-(next-4))/(4)*100,0,100):100,ready:false,detail:'Each awarded Advance opens an improvement prompt.'}}
 if(SYS.key==='blades'){const t=currentBladesTrack(),cur=bladesValue(t),th=bladesThreshold(t);return{primary:cur+' / '+th+' '+t+' XP',secondary:'Active track: '+t,next:Math.max(0,th-cur)+' XP remaining',status:cur>=th?'Advance ready':'Tracking',pct:clamp(cur/th*100,0,100),ready:cur>=th,detail:'Playbook 8; attributes 6; crew 10.'}}
 return{primary:'—',secondary:'—',next:'—',status:'Tracking',pct:0,ready:false,detail:''}}
function awardDefs(){switch(SYS.key){
 case'dnd':return[{id:'hostile',label:'Defeating or overcoming hostiles',amount:true},{id:'party',label:'Hostile encounter total ÷ party size',amount:true,party:true},{id:'quest',label:'Quest completion',amount:true},{id:'milestone',label:'Milestone XP award',amount:true},{id:'other',label:'Other DM-approved XP',amount:true}];
 case'pathfinder':return[{id:'minor',label:'Minor accomplishment — 10 XP',fixed:10},{id:'moderate',label:'Moderate accomplishment — 30 XP',fixed:30},{id:'major',label:'Major accomplishment — 80 XP',fixed:80},{id:'adversary',label:'Adversary by relative level',relative:true},{id:'simple',label:'Simple hazard by relative level',relative:true},{id:'complex',label:'Complex hazard by relative level',relative:true},{id:'custom',label:'Custom XP award',amount:true}];
 case'fate':return[{id:'minor',label:'Minor milestone'},{id:'significant',label:'Significant milestone'},{id:'major',label:'Major milestone'}];
 case'gurps':return[{id:'session',label:'Session award (0–5 CP)',amount:true,defaultAmount:1},{id:'bonus',label:'Bonus / mission character points',amount:true},{id:'custom',label:'Other GM-approved points',amount:true}];
 case'coc':return[{id:'development',label:'Resolve a checked skill improvement',development:true},{id:'phase',label:'Open full development phase',phase:true}];
 case'daggerheart':return[{id:'session',label:'Record one session toward milestone',fixed:1},{id:'milestone',label:'GM declares narrative milestone now',milestone:true},{id:'custom',label:'Add custom session progress',amount:true}];
 case'pbta':return[{id:'miss',label:'Mark XP from a miss / game trigger',fixed:1},{id:'trigger',label:'Mark one playbook XP trigger',fixed:1},{id:'session',label:'End-of-session XP marks',amount:true,defaultAmount:1},{id:'custom',label:'Custom playbook-approved XP',amount:true}];
 case'swade':return[{id:'advance',label:'Award one Advance',fixed:1},{id:'multiple',label:'Award multiple Advances',amount:true,defaultAmount:1},{id:'custom',label:'Custom setting-approved Advance count',amount:true,defaultAmount:1}];
 case'blades':return[{id:'desperate',label:'Desperate action roll — 1 XP',fixed:1,track:true,attributesOnly:true},{id:'eos1',label:'End-session trigger happened — 1 XP',fixed:1,track:true},{id:'eos2',label:'End-session trigger happened a lot — 2 XP',fixed:2,track:true},{id:'training',label:'Downtime training — 1 XP',fixed:1,track:true,noCrew:true},{id:'training2',label:'Downtime training with training upgrade — 2 XP',fixed:2,track:true,noCrew:true},{id:'crew1',label:'Crew trigger — 1 crew XP',fixed:1,forcedTrack:'crew'},{id:'crew2',label:'Crew trigger happened a lot — 2 crew XP',fixed:2,forcedTrack:'crew'},{id:'custom',label:'Custom XP award',amount:true,track:true}];
 default:return[]}}
function optionsHtml(list){return list.map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x.id)}">${esc(x.label)}</option>`).join('')}
function build(){if(byId('progression-advancement-v2'))return;const section=document.createElement('section');section.id='progression-advancement-v2';section.innerHTML=`<div class="pa-head"><div><div class="pa-kicker">System-correct progression automation</div><h2>${esc(SYS.title)}</h2><p class="pa-help" id="paHelp"></p></div><span class="pa-ready not" id="paReady">Tracking</span></div><div class="pa-summary"><div class="pa-metric"><span>Current</span><b id="paCurrent">—</b></div><div class="pa-metric"><span>Position</span><b id="paPosition">—</b></div><div class="pa-metric"><span>Next</span><b id="paNext">—</b></div><div class="pa-metric"><span>Status</span><b id="paStatus">—</b></div></div><div class="pa-progress" aria-label="Progress toward next advancement"><i id="paProgressBar"></i></div><div class="pa-grid"><div class="pa-card"><h3>Add XP or progression</h3><div class="pa-fields"><div class="pa-field wide"><label for="paAwardType">Award / progression event</label><select id="paAwardType">${optionsHtml(awardDefs())}</select></div><div id="paDynamicFields" class="pa-field wide"></div><div class="pa-field wide"><label for="paMemo">Session note or reason</label><input id="paMemo" placeholder="What earned this award or advancement?"></div></div><div class="pa-actions"><button type="button" class="pa-btn" id="paSubmit">Calculate & Submit</button><button type="button" class="pa-btn secondary" id="paOpenWizard">Open Advancement Wizard</button></div><p class="pa-note">The calculator never silently certifies optional-book or homebrew choices. Anything not unambiguously legal remains marked for DM/GM/MOL review.</p></div><div class="pa-card"><h3>Recent progression</h3><div class="pa-history" id="paHistory"></div><div class="pa-actions"><button type="button" class="pa-btn secondary" id="paExportHistory">Export progression JSON</button><button type="button" class="pa-btn danger" id="paUndo">Undo last award</button></div></div></div>`;
 const anchor=document.querySelector('main')||document.querySelector('.shell')||document.body;anchor.appendChild(section);
 const back=document.createElement('div');back.id='paAdvancementBackdrop';back.className='pa-backdrop';back.innerHTML='<div class="pa-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="paWizardTitle"><div class="pa-dialog-head"><div><div class="pa-kicker">Guided advancement</div><h2 id="paWizardTitle">Advancement Wizard</h2></div><button type="button" class="pa-close" id="paCloseWizard" aria-label="Close">×</button></div><div id="paWizardBody"></div></div>';document.body.appendChild(back);
 byId('paAwardType').addEventListener('change',renderDynamic);byId('paSubmit').addEventListener('click',submitAward);byId('paOpenWizard').addEventListener('click',()=>openWizard('manual'));byId('paCloseWizard').addEventListener('click',closeWizard);back.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===back)closeWizard()});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')closeWizard()});byId('paExportHistory').addEventListener('click',exportHistory);byId('paUndo').addEventListener('click',undoLast);renderDynamic();render();addJumpLink()}
function addJumpLink(){const menus=[...document.querySelectorAll('.cs-bubble-panel nav,.cs-bubble-panel,.jump-menu,.nav')];for(const m of menus){if(m.querySelector('[href="#progression-advancement-v2"]'))continue;const a=document.createElement('a');a.href='#progression-advancement-v2';a.textContent='XP & Advancement';a.className='pa-jump-link';a.addEventListener('click',()=>document.querySelector('.cs-bubble')?.classList.remove('open'));m.appendChild(a);break}}
function renderDynamic(){const def=awardDefs().find(x=>x.id===byId('paAwardType')?.value)||awardDefs()[0],root=byId('paDynamicFields');if(!root||!def)return;let h='';
 if(def.amount)h+=`<div class="pa-fields"><div class="pa-field"><label for="paAmount">Amount</label><input id="paAmount" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${def.defaultAmount??0}"></div>${def.party?'<div class="pa-field"><label for="paParty">Party size</label><input id="paParty" type="number" min="1" step="1" value="4"></div>':''}</div>`;
 if(def.relative)h+=`<div class="pa-field"><label for="paRelative">Challenge relative to party level</label><select id="paRelative">${[-4,-3,-2,-1,0,1,2,3,4].map(v=>`<option value="${v}">${v===0?'Equal level':v>0?'Party level +'+v:'Party level '+v}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`;
 if(def.track||def.forcedTrack)h+=`<div class="pa-field"><label for="paTrack">XP track</label><select id="paTrack" ${def.forcedTrack?'disabled':''}>${trackOptions(def).map(v=>`<option value="${v}" ${v===(def.forcedTrack||currentBladesTrack())?'selected':''}>${v[0].toUpperCase()+v.slice(1)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`;
 if(def.development)h+=developmentFields();
 if(SYS.key==='pathfinder')h+=`<div class="pa-field"><label for="paPfThreshold">Campaign level threshold</label><select id="paPfThreshold"><option value="1000">Standard — 1,000 XP</option><option value="800">Fast — 800 XP</option><option value="1200">Slow — 1,200 XP</option><option value="custom">Custom</option></select></div><div class="pa-field pa-hidden" id="paPfCustomWrap"><label for="paPfCustom">Custom threshold</label><input id="paPfCustom" type="number" min="1" value="${num(ensure().settings.pfThreshold,1000)}"></div>`;
 if(SYS.key==='daggerheart')h+=`<div class="pa-field"><label for="paDaggerTarget">Session reminder target</label><input id="paDaggerTarget" type="number" min="1" max="12" value="${num(ensure().settings.daggerSessions,3)}"></div>`;
 if(SYS.key==='pbta')h+=`<div class="pa-field"><label for="paPbtaThreshold">Exact playbook XP threshold</label><input id="paPbtaThreshold" type="number" min="1" max="30" value="${num(S().xp?.max||ensure().settings.pbtaThreshold,5)}"></div>`;
 root.innerHTML=h;
 byId('paPfThreshold')?.addEventListener('change',e=>byId('paPfCustomWrap')?.classList.toggle('pa-hidden',e.target.value!=='custom'))}
function trackOptions(def){let a=['playbook','insight','prowess','resolve','crew'];if(def.attributesOnly)a=['insight','prowess','resolve'];if(def.noCrew)a=a.filter(x=>x!=='crew');return a}
function developmentFields(){const skills=(S().skills||[]).filter(x=>x.checked&&!/cthulhu mythos/i.test(x.name||''));return `<div class="pa-fields"><div class="pa-field wide"><label for="paCocSkill">Checked skill</label><select id="paCocSkill">${skills.length?skills.map((x,i)=>`<option value="${(S().skills||[]).indexOf(x)}">${esc(x.name)} — ${num(x.value)}%</option>`).join(''):'<option value="">No eligible checked skills</option>'}</select></div><div class="pa-field"><label for="paCocRoll">Improvement roll (d100)</label><input id="paCocRoll" type="number" min="1" max="100" value="${Math.floor(Math.random()*100)+1}"></div><div class="pa-field"><label for="paCocGain">Increase (d10)</label><input id="paCocGain" type="number" min="1" max="10" value="${Math.floor(Math.random()*10)+1}"></div></div>`}
function calculateAward(def){let amount=num(byId('paAmount')?.value,def.fixed||0);if(def.fixed!=null)amount=def.fixed;if(def.party)amount=Math.floor(amount/Math.max(1,num(byId('paParty')?.value,1)));if(def.relative){const r=String(byId('paRelative')?.value||0);amount=(def.id==='simple'?PF_SIMPLE:def.id==='complex'?PF_COMPLEX:PF_ADVERSARY)[r]||0}return Math.max(0,Math.floor(amount))}
function hist(entry){const e=ensure();entry={id:'pa-'+Date.now()+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),at:new Date().toISOString(),...entry};e.history.unshift(entry);e.history=e.history.slice(0,250);return entry}
function submitAward(){const def=awardDefs().find(x=>x.id===byId('paAwardType').value),memo=byId('paMemo').value.trim(),s=S(),e=ensure();if(!def)return;
 if(SYS.key==='pathfinder'){const v=byId('paPfThreshold')?.value;if(v){e.settings.pfThreshold=v==='custom'?Math.max(1,num(byId('paPfCustom')?.value,1000)):num(v,1000)}}
 if(SYS.key==='daggerheart')e.settings.daggerSessions=Math.max(1,num(byId('paDaggerTarget')?.value,3));
 if(SYS.key==='pbta'){e.settings.pbtaThreshold=Math.max(1,num(byId('paPbtaThreshold')?.value,5));s.xp=s.xp||{};s.xp.max=e.settings.pbtaThreshold}
 if(SYS.key==='coc'){if(def.phase){openWizard('coc-phase');return}resolveCocSkill(def,memo);return}
 const amount=calculateAward(def);let before=clone(summary()),target='';
 if(SYS.key==='dnd'){s.profile=s.profile||{};s.profile.xp=Math.max(0,num(s.profile.xp)+amount)}
 else if(SYS.key==='pathfinder')e.xp=Math.max(0,num(e.xp)+amount);
 else if(SYS.key==='fate'){e.milestones=e.milestones||{minor:0,significant:0,major:0};e.milestones[def.id]=num(e.milestones[def.id])+1;target=def.id}
 else if(SYS.key==='gurps')e.gurpsEarned=Math.max(0,num(e.gurpsEarned)+amount);
 else if(SYS.key==='daggerheart'){if(def.milestone)e.daggerSessions=Math.max(num(e.daggerSessions),num(e.settings.daggerSessions,3));else e.daggerSessions=Math.max(0,num(e.daggerSessions)+amount)}
 else if(SYS.key==='pbta'){s.xp=s.xp||{current:0,max:e.settings.pbtaThreshold};s.xp.current=Math.max(0,num(s.xp.current)+amount)}
 else if(SYS.key==='swade'){const count=Math.max(1,amount||1);e.swadeAdvances=Math.max(num(e.swadeAdvances),(s.advances||[]).length)+count;s.advances=Array.isArray(s.advances)?s.advances:[];for(let i=0;i<count;i++)s.advances.push({name:`Advance ${s.advances.length+1}`,rank:swadeRank(e.swadeAdvances-count+i+1),type:'Pending choice',notes:memo});s.profile=s.profile||{};s.profile.rank=swadeRank(e.swadeAdvances)}
 else if(SYS.key==='blades'){target=def.forcedTrack||byId('paTrack')?.value||'playbook';e.lastTrack=target;if(target==='crew'){s.crew=s.crew||{};s.crew.crewXp=Math.max(0,num(s.crew.crewXp)+amount)}else{s.xp=s.xp||{};s.xp[target]=Math.max(0,num(s.xp[target])+amount)}}
 const after=summary();hist({kind:'award',system:SYS.key,awardType:def.id,label:def.label,amount,target,memo,before,after});save();render();if(SYS.key==='fate'||SYS.key==='swade'||SYS.key==='daggerheart'&&def.milestone||after.ready)openWizard('automatic')}
function resolveCocSkill(def,memo){const s=S(),idx=num(byId('paCocSkill')?.value,-1),skill=s.skills?.[idx];if(!skill){alert('No eligible checked skill is selected.');return}const roll=clamp(num(byId('paCocRoll')?.value),1,100),gain=clamp(num(byId('paCocGain')?.value),1,10),old=num(skill.value),success=roll>old;if(success)skill.value=Math.min(99,old+gain);skill.checked=false;hist({kind:'development',system:'coc',skill:skill.name,roll,gain:success?gain:0,oldValue:old,newValue:num(skill.value),success,memo});save();render();alert(success?`${skill.name} improved from ${old}% to ${skill.value}%.`:`${skill.name} did not improve; the check is cleared.`);renderDynamic()}
function catalog(){try{return window.RulesHomebrewEngineAPI?.getCatalog?.()||[]}catch(_){return[]}}
function matchCatalog(patterns){const pats=patterns.map(x=>String(x).toLowerCase());return catalog().filter(x=>pats.some(p=>String(x.category||'').toLowerCase().includes(p))).slice(0,900)}
function catSelect(id,label,patterns,wide=false){const rows=matchCatalog(patterns);return `<div class="pa-wizard-field ${wide?'wide':''}"><label for="${id}">${esc(label)}</label><select id="${id}" data-catalog-choice><option value="">— No selection / enter manually —</option>${rows.map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x.id)}">${esc(x.name)} — ${esc(x.source||x.category)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`}
function textField(id,label,placeholder='',wide=false){return `<div class="pa-wizard-field ${wide?'wide':''}"><label for="${id}">${esc(label)}</label><input id="${id}" data-text-choice placeholder="${esc(placeholder)}"></div>`}
function areaField(id,label,placeholder='',wide=true){return `<div class="pa-wizard-field ${wide?'wide':''}"><label for="${id}">${esc(label)}</label><textarea id="${id}" data-text-choice placeholder="${esc(placeholder)}"></textarea></div>`}
function wizardSpec(){const s=S(),e=ensure(),sum=summary();let title='Advancement Wizard',intro='',fields='',checks=[];
 if(SYS.key==='dnd'){const char=totalDndLevel(),target=Math.min(20,char+1);title=`D&D Level ${target} Choices`;intro=`Your cumulative XP supports level ${levelForXp(num(s.profile?.xp))}. Complete one class level at a time.`;checks=['Choose the class receiving the level or a legal multiclass.','Apply the new Hit Die and HP increase.','Review class/subclass features and resource changes.','Review feat or Ability Score Improvement choices when granted.','Update spells known/prepared, slots, attacks, and proficiency-based values.'];fields=`${catSelect('paWClass','Class receiving this level',['class'])}${textField('paWExistingClass','Existing/custom class or multiclass note','Example: Artificer 6 or Bard 4')}${catSelect('paWSubclass','Subclass choice, when granted',['subclass'])}${catSelect('paWFeat','Feat / boon / ASI replacement, when granted',['feat','boon'])}${textField('paWSkill','Skill proficiency, expertise, tool, or language choice','Record every choice offered by the class')}${catSelect('paWSpell','Spell choice or preparation change',['spell'])}${catSelect('paWAttack','Attack, maneuver, mastery, or fighting option',['attack','maneuver','mastery','fighting'])}${areaField('paWNotes','Class feature and multiclass prerequisite notes','Record HP method, new features, spell-slot changes, prerequisites, and DM rulings.')}`}
 else if(SYS.key==='pathfinder'){const old=Math.max(1,num(s.profile?.level,1)),nl=Math.min(20,old+1),due=[];if(nl%2===0)due.push('Class feat','Skill feat');if(nl>=3&&nl%2===1)due.push('Skill increase');if([3,7,11,15,19].includes(nl))due.push('General feat');if([5,9,13,17].includes(nl))due.push('Ancestry feat');if([5,10,15,20].includes(nl))due.push('Four ability boosts');title=`Pathfinder Level ${nl} Choices`;intro=`Spend ${num(e.settings.pfThreshold,1000)} XP, carry excess XP, and apply the level ${nl} class table. Scheduled prompts: ${due.join(', ')||'class features and spell progression'}.`;checks=['Increase level and every proficiency modifier that includes level.','Apply ancestry and class HP for the new level.','Follow the exact class table for class features, proficiency increases, spell ranks, slots, and repertoire/preparations.','Check feat prerequisites and archetype/free-archetype campaign rules.','Review equipment, formulas, and retraining.'];fields=`${nl%2===0?catSelect('paWClassFeat','Class feat',['class feat','feat']):''}${nl%2===0?catSelect('paWSkillFeat','Skill feat',['skill feat']):''}${[3,7,11,15,19].includes(nl)?catSelect('paWGeneralFeat','General feat',['general feat']):''}${[5,9,13,17].includes(nl)?catSelect('paWAncestryFeat','Ancestry feat',['ancestry feat']):''}${nl>=3&&nl%2===1?textField('paWSkillIncrease','Skill proficiency increase','Skill: trained → expert, etc.') :''}${[5,10,15,20].includes(nl)?areaField('paWBoosts','Four ability boosts','List the four different abilities and resulting modifiers.') :''}${catSelect('paWArchetype','Archetype / dedication / multiclass feat, if applicable',['archetype','dedication'])}${catSelect('paWSpell','New spell, focus spell, or ritual',['spell','ritual'])}${areaField('paWNotes','Class-table and other choices','Record class feature selections, spell-slot/repertoire changes, formulas, and GM rulings.')}`}
 else if(SYS.key==='fate'){const kind=e.pendingMilestone||'minor';title=`Fate ${kind[0].toUpperCase()+kind.slice(1)} Milestone`;intro=kind==='minor'?'Choose one character adjustment.':kind==='significant'?'Gain the minor-milestone option plus one skill point.':'Gain minor and significant benefits, plus the major-milestone benefits.';checks=kind==='minor'?['Swap the ratings of two skills or replace one Average (+1) skill.','Change one stunt, buy a stunt if refresh permits, or rename a non-high-concept aspect.']:kind==='significant'?['Choose one minor milestone adjustment.','Spend or bank one skill point while respecting skill columns.']:['Choose minor and significant milestone benefits.','Choose +1 refresh, raise the skill cap/skill above it, or rename the high concept.'];fields=`<div class="pa-wizard-field"><label for="paWFateChoice">Milestone choice</label><select id="paWFateChoice" data-text-choice><option>Swap two skill ratings</option><option>Replace an Average (+1) skill</option><option>Change or buy a stunt</option><option>Rename an aspect</option>${kind!=='minor'?'<option>Gain or bank one skill point</option>':''}${kind==='major'?'<option>Gain +1 refresh</option><option>Raise skill cap / advance above cap</option><option>Rename high concept</option>':''}</select></div>${catSelect('paWStunt','Stunt selection, if applicable',['stunt'])}${textField('paWSkills','Skill/aspect changes','Describe old → new')}${areaField('paWNotes','Story justification and final changes')}`}
 else if(SYS.key==='gurps'){title='GURPS Character Point Spending';intro='Spend earned character points only on changes allowed by the campaign and GM. There is no universal level-up threshold.';checks=['Confirm the point cost from the exact owned rule source.','Check training time, self-study, teacher, supernatural, social, and campaign availability restrictions.','Do not exceed available unspent points.','Recalculate attributes, secondary characteristics, skills, techniques, spells, defenses, and encumbrance as affected.'];fields=`<div class="pa-wizard-field"><label for="paWGurpsType">Purchase type</label><select id="paWGurpsType" data-text-choice><option>Attribute or secondary characteristic</option><option>Skill</option><option>Technique</option><option>Advantage</option><option>Perk</option><option>Spell</option><option>Buy off disadvantage</option><option>Other GM-approved trait</option></select></div>${catSelect('paWGurpsCatalog','Catalog option',['advantage','disadvantage','skill','spell','technique','perk'])}${textField('paWGurpsName','Trait / skill / spell name','Manual entry is allowed')}${textField('paWGurpsCost','Character point cost','Whole number cost')}${areaField('paWNotes','Training, prerequisites, and resulting calculations')}`}
 else if(SYS.key==='coc'){title='Call of Cthulhu Development Phase';intro='Resolve each checked eligible skill separately. Cthulhu Mythos is not improved by ordinary experience checks.';checks=['Select a checked skill.','Roll 1d100; improvement succeeds only when the roll exceeds the current skill value.','On success, add 1d10 percentage points.','Clear the check whether or not the skill improves.','Repeat for each checked eligible skill.'];fields=`<div class="pa-warning">Close this wizard and use “Resolve a checked skill improvement” in the calculator. It automatically applies the roll and clears the check.</div>`}
 else if(SYS.key==='daggerheart'){const old=Math.max(1,num(s.profile?.level,1)),nl=Math.min(10,old+1),tier=[2,5,8].includes(nl);title=`Daggerheart Level ${nl} Advancement`;intro='Choose two advancement slots, gain the level threshold and domain-card benefits, and review tier achievements when entering a new tier.';checks=['Increase level and the damage thresholds according to the level-up rules.','Choose exactly two advancement options; an option can normally be selected only where its slot permits.','Choose the new domain card available for the level.','At levels 2, 5, and 8, apply the tier achievement.','Multiclassing is available only when the level and table rules permit it.'];fields=`<div class="pa-wizard-field"><label for="paWDag1">Advancement slot 1</label><select id="paWDag1" data-text-choice>${daggerOptions(nl)}</select></div><div class="pa-wizard-field"><label for="paWDag2">Advancement slot 2</label><select id="paWDag2" data-text-choice>${daggerOptions(nl)}</select></div>${catSelect('paWDomain','Domain card gained for this level',['domain card','domain'])}${catSelect('paWSubclass','Subclass upgrade, if chosen',['subclass'])}${nl>=5?catSelect('paWMulticlass','Multiclass option, costs both slots when applicable',['class']):''}${tier?textField('paWTier','Tier achievement','Record the level '+nl+' tier achievement'):''}${areaField('paWNotes','Threshold, experience, feature, and other choices')}`}
 else if(SYS.key==='pbta'){title='PbtA Playbook Improvement';intro='Use only the improvement list printed by the exact game and playbook. The adapter intentionally does not invent a universal PbtA level system.';checks=['Confirm the XP threshold from the exact playbook.','Choose one available improvement and mark it as used if the playbook limits repeats.','Apply stat, move, relationship, resource, retirement, or playbook changes exactly as written.','If selecting a move from another playbook, preserve all prerequisites and limits.'];fields=`<div class="pa-wizard-field"><label for="paWPbtaType">Improvement type</label><select id="paWPbtaType" data-text-choice><option>Increase a stat</option><option>Take a new playbook move</option><option>Take a move from another playbook</option><option>Gain a crew, ally, holding, or resource</option><option>Unlock an advanced improvement</option><option>Retire safely / change playbook</option><option>Other exact-game improvement</option></select></div>${catSelect('paWMove','Move or playbook option',['move','playbook'])}${textField('paWExact','Exact improvement wording / name','From the owned playbook')}${areaField('paWNotes','Stat, move, relationship, or fiction changes')}`}
 else if(SYS.key==='swade'){const n=Math.max(num(e.swadeAdvances),(s.advances||[]).length),rank=swadeRank(n);title=`SWADE ${rank} Advance`;intro='Choose one improvement allowed by the current Rank, prerequisites, and setting rules.';checks=['Check Rank, Trait, ancestry, Arcane Background, and setting prerequisites.','Choose an eligible Edge; raise one skill at/above its linked Attribute; raise two skills below linked Attributes; buy a new skill at d4; or increase an Attribute when allowed.','Update derived statistics, weapons, Powers, Power Points, Pace, Parry, Toughness, and notes as affected.'];fields=`<div class="pa-wizard-field"><label for="paWSwadeType">Advance use</label><select id="paWSwadeType" data-text-choice><option>Gain an Edge</option><option>Increase one skill at/above linked Attribute</option><option>Increase two skills below linked Attributes</option><option>Buy a new skill at d4</option><option>Increase an Attribute when allowed</option><option>Remove/reduce a Hindrance with GM approval</option><option>Other setting-approved Advance</option></select></div>${catSelect('paWEdge','Edge / Power / option',['edge','power'])}${textField('paWSkill','Skill or Attribute changes','Example: Fighting d8 → d10')}${areaField('paWNotes','Prerequisites and recalculated values')}`}
 else if(SYS.key==='blades'){const t=currentBladesTrack();title=`Blades ${t[0].toUpperCase()+t.slice(1)} Advance`;intro=t==='playbook'?'Clear the playbook XP track and choose a special ability.':t==='crew'?'Clear crew XP and choose one crew special ability or two crew upgrades.':`Clear the ${t} XP track and add one action dot under ${t}.`;checks=t==='playbook'?['Choose one additional special ability from the playbook or another allowed source.','Explain how the new ability enters the fiction.']:t==='crew'?['Choose one crew special ability or two crew upgrades.','Apply crew profits/stash and explain the fictional development.']:['Choose one action under the filled attribute.','Increase it by one dot without exceeding the game limit.'];fields=t==='playbook'?`${catSelect('paWAbility','Special ability',['special ability','ability'])}${areaField('paWNotes','How the ability was obtained')}`:t==='crew'?`${textField('paWCrewChoice','Crew ability or upgrade 1')}${textField('paWCrewChoice2','Crew upgrade 2, if applicable')}${areaField('paWNotes','Crew development and profits')}`:`${textField('paWAction','Action gaining one dot',t+' action')}${areaField('paWNotes','Training or fictional explanation')}`}
 return{title,intro,checks,fields}}
function daggerOptions(level){let opts=['Increase two unmarked traits by +1','Gain +1 Hit Point slot','Gain +1 Stress slot','Increase two Experiences by +1','Gain a new Experience at +2','Gain +1 Evasion','Upgrade subclass card','Choose a new domain card'];if(level>=5)opts.push('Multiclass — costs both advancement slots');opts.push('Increase Proficiency — costs both advancement slots when available');return opts.map(x=>`<option>${esc(x)}</option>`).join('')}
function openWizard(reason){const e=ensure(),sum=summary();if(SYS.key==='fate'&&reason!=='manual')e.pendingMilestone=byId('paAwardType')?.value||'minor';const w=wizardSpec();byId('paWizardTitle').textContent=w.title;byId('paWizardBody').innerHTML=`<p>${esc(w.intro)}</p><ul class="pa-checklist">${w.checks.map(x=>`<li>${esc(x)}</li>`).join('')}</ul><div class="pa-warning">Choices from optional books or homebrew remain pending DM/GM/MOL review until approved. The wizard records selections and applies only system-safe automatic changes.</div><div class="pa-wizard-grid">${w.fields}</div><div class="pa-actions"><button type="button" class="pa-btn" id="paApplyWizard">Record Choices & Apply Advancement</button>${SYS.key==='dnd'?'<button type="button" class="pa-btn secondary" id="paNativeDnd">Open Native D&D Level-Up Builder</button>':''}<button type="button" class="pa-btn secondary" id="paCancelWizard">Close Without Applying</button></div>`;byId('paAdvancementBackdrop').classList.add('open');document.body.style.overflow='hidden';byId('paApplyWizard').onclick=applyWizard;byId('paCancelWizard').onclick=closeWizard;if(byId('paNativeDnd'))byId('paNativeDnd').onclick=openNativeDnd;setTimeout(()=>byId('paApplyWizard')?.focus(),30)}
function closeWizard(){byId('paAdvancementBackdrop')?.classList.remove('open');document.body.style.overflow=''}
function collectWizard(){const root=byId('paWizardBody'),out={catalog:[],text:{}};root.querySelectorAll('[data-catalog-choice]').forEach(el=>{if(el.value)out.catalog.push({id:el.value,label:el.options[el.selectedIndex]?.text||el.value})});root.querySelectorAll('[data-text-choice]').forEach(el=>{if(String(el.value||'').trim())out.text[el.id]=String(el.value).trim()});return out}
function applyCatalogChoices(ch,newLevel){const api=window.RulesHomebrewEngineAPI;if(!api)return;for(const x of ch.catalog)try{api.addById(x.id,'Pending DM/GM/MOL review')}catch(_){};for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(ch.text)){if(!v)continue;if(['paWNotes','paWSkills','paWBoosts'].includes(k))continue;try{api.addCustom({name:v,category:'Advancement Choice',level:newLevel||'',notes:'Recorded by the progression wizard. Verify legality and calculations with the DM/GM/MOL.'})}catch(_){}}}
function applyWizard(){const s=S(),e=ensure(),ch=collectWizard(),record={kind:'advancement',system:SYS.key,at:new Date().toISOString(),choices:ch};
 if(SYS.key==='dnd'){record.fromLevel=totalDndLevel();record.toLevel=Math.min(20,record.fromLevel+1);record.status='Choices recorded; complete native class-level workflow';applyCatalogChoices(ch,record.toLevel);e.pending.push(record);hist(record);save();openNativeDnd();closeWizard();return}
 if(SYS.key==='pathfinder'){const th=Math.max(1,num(e.settings.pfThreshold,1000)),old=Math.max(1,num(s.profile?.level,1));if(num(e.xp)<th){if(!confirm('The XP track is not full. Record these choices as a planned advancement anyway?'))return;record.status='planned'}else{e.xp=Math.max(0,num(e.xp)-th);s.profile.level=Math.min(20,old+1);record.fromLevel=old;record.toLevel=s.profile.level;record.status='applied'}applyCatalogChoices(ch,record.toLevel)}
 else if(SYS.key==='fate'){record.milestone=e.pendingMilestone||'minor';s.advancement=s.advancement||{};s.advancement.milestone=record.milestone[0].toUpperCase()+record.milestone.slice(1);s.advancement.changes=[s.advancement.changes,Object.values(ch.text).join('; ')].filter(Boolean).join('\n');applyCatalogChoices(ch,'')}
 else if(SYS.key==='gurps'){const cost=Math.max(0,num(byId('paWGurpsCost')?.value));const available=Math.max(0,num(e.gurpsEarned)-num(e.settings.gurpsSpent));if(cost>available){alert(`This purchase costs ${cost} CP, but only ${available} CP are unspent.`);return}e.settings.gurpsSpent=num(e.settings.gurpsSpent)+cost;record.cost=cost;applyCatalogChoices(ch,'')}
 else if(SYS.key==='daggerheart'){const old=Math.max(1,num(s.profile?.level,1)),tar=Math.max(1,num(e.settings.daggerSessions,3));if(num(e.daggerSessions)<tar&&!confirm('The narrative milestone reminder is not full. Did the GM declare a milestone anyway?'))return;s.profile.level=Math.min(10,old+1);e.daggerSessions=Math.max(0,num(e.daggerSessions)-tar);record.fromLevel=old;record.toLevel=s.profile.level;applyCatalogChoices(ch,record.toLevel)}
 else if(SYS.key==='pbta'){const max=Math.max(1,num(s.xp?.max||e.settings.pbtaThreshold,5));if(num(s.xp?.current)<max){if(!confirm('The XP track is not full. Record this as a planned improvement?'))return;record.status='planned'}else{s.xp.current=Math.max(0,num(s.xp.current)-max);record.status='applied'}s.advancements=Array.isArray(s.advancements)?s.advancements:[];s.advancements.push({name:Object.values(ch.text)[0]||ch.catalog[0]?.label||'Playbook improvement',notes:Object.values(ch.text).slice(1).join('; ')});applyCatalogChoices(ch,'')}
 else if(SYS.key==='swade'){const last=s.advances?.[s.advances.length-1];if(last){last.type=Object.values(ch.text)[0]||ch.catalog[0]?.label||'Advance choice';last.notes=[last.notes,Object.values(ch.text).slice(1).join('; ')].filter(Boolean).join(' — ')}applyCatalogChoices(ch,swadeRank(Math.max(num(e.swadeAdvances),(s.advances||[]).length)))}
 else if(SYS.key==='blades'){const t=currentBladesTrack(),th=bladesThreshold(t),cur=bladesValue(t);if(cur<th){if(!confirm('This XP track is not full. Record the choice as planned?'))return;record.status='planned'}else{if(t==='crew')s.crew.crewXp=Math.max(0,cur-th);else s.xp[t]=Math.max(0,cur-th);record.status='applied'}applyCatalogChoices(ch,'')}
 else if(SYS.key==='coc'){closeWizard();return}
 record.choices=ch;hist(record);e.choices.unshift(record);save();closeWizard();render()}
function openNativeDnd(){const btn=byId('levelUpBtn')||[...document.querySelectorAll('button')].find(b=>/level up/i.test(b.textContent||''));if(btn){closeWizard();btn.click();btn.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'center'})}else alert('The native D&D level-up builder could not be located. Your choices were still recorded for review.')}
function render(){const root=byId('progression-advancement-v2');if(!root)return;const x=summary();byId('paCurrent').textContent=x.primary;byId('paPosition').textContent=x.secondary;byId('paNext').textContent=x.next;byId('paStatus').textContent=x.status;byId('paHelp').textContent=x.detail;byId('paProgressBar').style.width=clamp(x.pct,0,100)+'%';const ready=byId('paReady');ready.textContent=x.ready?'Advancement ready':'Tracking';ready.classList.toggle('not',!x.ready);const e=ensure(),h=byId('paHistory');h.innerHTML=e.history.length?e.history.slice(0,12).map(v=>`<div class="pa-history-entry"><b>${esc(v.label||v.kind||'Progression')}</b>${v.amount!=null?` — ${esc(v.amount)}${v.target?' to '+esc(v.target):''}`:''}${v.skill?` — ${esc(v.skill)}${v.success?' improved':' no increase'}`:''}${v.memo?`<div>${esc(v.memo)}</div>`:''}<time>${esc(new Date(v.at).toLocaleString())}</time></div>`).join(''):'<div class="pa-note">No XP or advancement events recorded yet.</div>'}
function undoLast(){const e=ensure(),v=e.history.shift();if(!v){alert('There is no progression event to undo.');return}const s=S();if(v.kind==='award'){
 if(SYS.key==='dnd')s.profile.xp=Math.max(0,num(s.profile.xp)-num(v.amount));
 else if(SYS.key==='pathfinder')e.xp=Math.max(0,num(e.xp)-num(v.amount));
 else if(SYS.key==='fate'&&v.target&&e.milestones)e.milestones[v.target]=Math.max(0,num(e.milestones[v.target])-1);
 else if(SYS.key==='gurps')e.gurpsEarned=Math.max(0,num(e.gurpsEarned)-num(v.amount));
 else if(SYS.key==='daggerheart')e.daggerSessions=Math.max(0,num(e.daggerSessions)-num(v.amount));
 else if(SYS.key==='pbta')s.xp.current=Math.max(0,num(s.xp.current)-num(v.amount));
 else if(SYS.key==='swade'){const c=Math.max(1,num(v.amount));s.advances.splice(Math.max(0,s.advances.length-c),c);e.swadeAdvances=Math.max(0,num(e.swadeAdvances)-c);s.profile.rank=swadeRank(e.swadeAdvances)}
 else if(SYS.key==='blades'){const t=v.target||'playbook';if(t==='crew')s.crew.crewXp=Math.max(0,num(s.crew.crewXp)-num(v.amount));else s.xp[t]=Math.max(0,num(s.xp[t])-num(v.amount))}}
 else{e.history.unshift(v);alert('Only the most recent award can be undone automatically. Advancement choices remain preserved.');return}save();render()}
function exportHistory(){const data={schema:'ttrpg.progression-engine.v2',system:SYS,exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),summary:summary(),progression:clone(ensure())},blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(data,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download=(S().profile?.name||'character').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-')+'-'+SYS.key+'-progression.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000)}
function init(){ensure();build();setInterval(()=>{if(byId('progression-advancement-v2'))render()},1500);window.ProgressionAdvancementEngineV2={system:SYS,summary,submitAward,openWizard,render,awardDefs,getState:()=>clone(ensure())}}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
})();
</script>
<!-- PROGRESSION_ADVANCEMENT_ENGINE_V2_END -->
<script id="tablegateArtDownloadsV8">
(()=>{'use strict';
const q=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);const state=()=>{try{return window.__SHEET_TEST_API__?.getState?.()||window.state||{}}catch(_){return {}}};
const slug=v=>String(v||'character').trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi,'_').replace(/^_+|_+$/g,'')||'character';
function profile(){const s=state();return s.profile||s.character?.profile||{}}
function source(which){const p=profile(),s=state();if(which==='one')return p.portrait||p.characterArtOne||p.artPrimary||s.portrait||q('#portraitPreview,#portraitImg')?.src||'';if(which==='two')return p.secondaryArt||p.characterArtTwo||p.artSecondary||s.secondaryArt||q('#secondaryArtPreview,#secondImg')?.src||'';return p.tokenArt||s.tokenArt||q('#tokenArtImg')?.src||''}
function ext(data){const m=String(data).match(/^data:image\/([^;,]+)/i);const x=(m?.[1]||'png').toLowerCase();return x.includes('jpeg')||x==='jpg'?'jpg':x.includes('svg')?'svg':x.includes('webp')?'webp':'png'}
function name(){const p=profile();return slug(p.characterName||p.name||p.investigatorName||p.alias||'character')}
function download(data,file){if(!data)return false;const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=data;a.download=file;document.body.append(a);a.click();a.remove();return true}
async function renderedToken(){const data=source('token');if(!data)return false;const settings=state()?.studioAutomation?.artwork?.token||{};const img=new Image();await new Promise((ok,no)=>{img.onload=ok;img.onerror=no;img.src=data});const size=1024,c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=size;const x=c.getContext('2d');x.clearRect(0,0,size,size);x.save();x.beginPath();x.arc(size/2,size/2,size*.43,0,Math.PI*2);x.clip();const z=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(settings.zoom)||1));const scale=Math.max(size/img.width,size/img.height)*z;const w=img.width*scale,h=img.height*scale;const ox=(Number(settings.x)||0)/100*size,oy=(Number(settings.y)||0)/100*size;x.drawImage(img,(size-w)/2+ox,(size-h)/2+oy,w,h);x.restore();x.lineWidth=Math.max(8,Math.min(80,(Number(settings.borderWidth)||14)*2));x.strokeStyle=settings.borderColor||'#c9a84c';x.beginPath();x.arc(size/2,size/2,size*.43,0,Math.PI*2);x.stroke();return download(c.toDataURL('image/png'),`${name()}_token.png`)}
function install(){if(q('#tablegateArtDownloadBar'))return;const host=q('.art-actions')||q('#artwork .module-body,#artwork .panel-body,#studio-artwork .module-body')||document.body;const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.id='tablegateArtDownloadBar';bar.className='art-actions tablegate-art-downloads';bar.innerHTML='<button type="button" data-art-download="one">Download Full Art 1</button><button type="button" data-art-download="two">Download Full Art 2</button><button type="button" data-art-download="token">Download Token</button><button type="button" data-art-download="all">Download All Art</button>';host.append(bar);bar.addEventListener('click',async e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-art-download]');if(!b)return;const which=b.dataset.artDownload;let count=0;if(which==='one'||which==='all'){const d=source('one');if(d){download(d,`${name()}_full_art_1.${ext(d)}`);count++}}if(which==='two'||which==='all'){const d=source('two');if(d){download(d,`${name()}_full_art_2.${ext(d)}`);count++}}if(which==='token'||which==='all'){try{if(await renderedToken())count++}catch(_){const d=source('token');if(d){download(d,`${name()}_token_source.${ext(d)}`);count++}}}if(!count)alert('No saved character artwork is available yet.')});}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(install,180),{once:true});else setTimeout(install,180);
})();
</script>
<script id="tablegateCharacterBridge">
(()=>{'use strict';
const context=window.__TABLEGATE_EMBEDDED_CONTEXT__||{};
const post=(type,payload={})=>{try{parent.postMessage({type,...payload,characterId:context.characterId,context},'*')}catch(_){}};
const postTransfer=(type,payload={},transfers=[])=>{try{parent.postMessage({type,...payload,characterId:context.characterId,context},'*',transfers)}catch(_){post(type,payload)}};
const defaultExpression=()=>{const id=String(context.sheetId||'').toLowerCase();if(id.includes('fate'))return'4d6';if(id.includes('gurps'))return'3d6';if(id.includes('cthulhu'))return'1d100';if(id.includes('daggerheart'))return'2d12';if(id.includes('pbta'))return'2d6';if(id.includes('blades'))return'1d6';if(id.includes('swade'))return'1d6+1d6';return'1d20'};
const compact=v=>String(v||'').replace(/\s+/g,'');
const diceFrom=button=>{const text=[button.textContent,button.title,button.getAttribute('aria-label'),button.dataset.dice,button.dataset.roll,button.dataset.expression].filter(Boolean).join(' ');const exact=text.match(/\b(?:\d{1,2}d(?:4|6|8|9|10|12|20|100))(?:\s*[+\-]\s*\d+)?\b/i);if(exact)return compact(exact[0]);const scope=button.closest('.attack-card,.spell-card,.repeat-row,.list-card,.module,.panel,section,article,form')||button.parentElement;if(scope){const value=[...scope.querySelectorAll('input,select,[data-dice],[data-roll],[data-expression]')].map(el=>el.dataset.dice||el.dataset.roll||el.dataset.expression||el.value||'').find(v=>/^\d{1,2}d(?:4|6|8|9|10|12|20|100)(?:[+\-]\d+)?$/i.test(compact(v)));if(value)return compact(value)}return defaultExpression()};
const excluded=text=>/(stat generation|generate stats|randomi[sz]e stats|ability score generation|roll abilities|reroll ability|reroll stat|generate character|random character|point buy|standard array)/i.test(text);
const rollish=text=>/(^|\b)(roll|attack|damage|initiative|skill|save|saving throw|check|test|move|action|contest|cast|heal|recovery|resist|fortune|fear|hope)(\b|$)/i.test(text);
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(!context.sessionMode)return;const button=event.target.closest('button,[role="button"]');if(!button)return;const label=[button.textContent,button.title,button.getAttribute('aria-label'),button.dataset.action,button.dataset.roll].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim();if(!label||excluded(label)||!rollish(label)||button.closest('.cs-bubble-panel,#unifiedAssistBar,.assist-modal,#rules-homebrew-engine,#tgUniversalImportDock'))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();post('TABLEGATE_SHEET_ROLL_REQUEST',{action:label.slice(0,180),label:label.slice(0,180),expression:diceFrom(button),systemId:context.sheetId})},true);
let saveTimer=0;
const readState=()=>{try{return window.__SHEET_TEST_API__?.getState?.()||null}catch(_){return null}};
const sendState=()=>{clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=setTimeout(()=>{const sheetState=readState();let appearance=null;try{appearance=window.__CHARACTER_SHEET_APPEARANCE_API__?.getState?.()||null}catch(_){}if(sheetState)post('TABLEGATE_SHEET_STATE',{sheetState,appearance,title:document.title})},260)};
function dndSelectionRepair(event){if(!String(context.sheetId||'').includes('09_dnd'))return;const id=event?.target?.id;if(!['edition','raceSelect','raceWriteIn','raceOption','raceOptionWriteIn'].includes(id))return;setTimeout(()=>{const api=window.__SHEET_TEST_API__,s=readState();if(!api||!s?.profile)return;const p=s.profile;if(id==='edition'){p.edition=event.target.value;p.race='';p.raceWriteIn='';p.raceOption='';p.raceOptionWriteIn=''}if(id==='raceSelect'){p.race=event.target.value;p.raceWriteIn=p.race==='__writein__'?p.raceWriteIn:'';p.raceOption='';p.raceOptionWriteIn='';if(s.abilityRolls)s.abilityRolls.signature=''}if(id==='raceWriteIn'&&p.race==='__writein__')p.raceWriteIn=event.target.value;if(id==='raceOption'){p.raceOption=event.target.value;p.raceOptionWriteIn=p.raceOption==='__writein__'?p.raceOptionWriteIn:''}if(id==='raceOptionWriteIn'&&p.raceOption==='__writein__')p.raceOptionWriteIn=event.target.value;api.setState(s);sendState();post('TABLEGATE_SELECTION_AUDIT',{systemId:context.sheetId,field:id,value:event.target.value})},0)}
function installImporter(){if(document.getElementById('tgUniversalImportDock'))return;const dock=document.createElement('div');dock.id='tgUniversalImportDock';dock.setAttribute('role','region');dock.setAttribute('aria-label','Universal character import');dock.innerHTML='<button type="button" + 14px));display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:8px;border:1px solid rgba(98,224,255,.48);border-radius:999px;background:rgba(10,18,26,.94);box-shadow:0 12px 32px input=dock.querySelector('#tgUniversalImportFile'),button=dock.querySelector('#tgUniversalImportButton'),status=dock.querySelector('#tgUniversalImportStatus');button.onclick=()=>input.click();input.onchange=async()=>{const f.arrayBuffer();postTransfer('TABLEGATE_UNIVERSAL_IMPORT_FILE',{file:{name:f.name,type:f.type,size:f.size,lastModified:f.lastModified,buffer},targetSystemId:context.sheetId},[buffer]);status.textContent='Sent to TableGate import review.'}catch(err){status.textContent='Could not read file.'}finally{input.value=''}}}

function dependentSelectionRepair(event){const id=String(event?.target?.id||'');if(!id)return;const api=window.__SHEET_TEST_API__,s=readState();if(!api||!s)return;const p=s.profile||{};const lower=String(context.sheetId||'').toLowerCase();let touched=false;
 const clear=(keys)=>{for(const k of keys)if(k in p){p[k]='';touched=true}};
 if(lower.includes('pathfinder')&&/ancestry/i.test(id)){clear(['heritage','heritageWriteIn']);}
 if(lower.includes('daggerheart')&&/ancestry/i.test(id)){clear(['ancestryOption','ancestryOptionWriteIn']);}
 if(lower.includes('pbta')&&/playbook/i.test(id)){clear(['playbookOption','playbookWriteIn']);}
 if(lower.includes('swade')&&/(ancestry|race)/i.test(id)){clear(['ancestryOption','raceOption']);}
 if(lower.includes('blades')&&/heritage/i.test(id)){clear(['heritageDetail','heritageWriteIn']);}
 if(lower.includes('cthulhu')&&/occupation/i.test(id)){clear(['occupationSpecialty','occupationWriteIn']);}
 if(touched){api.setState(s);sendState();post('TABLEGATE_SELECTION_AUDIT',{systemId:context.sheetId,field:id,value:event.target.value})}}
document.addEventListener('input',sendState,true);document.addEventListener('change',event=>{dndSelectionRepair(event);dependentSelectionRepair(event);sendState()},true);addEventListener('beforeunload',sendState);addEventListener('message',event=>{const d=event.data||{};if(d.type==='TABLEGATE_APPLY_IMPORTED_STATE'&&(!d.characterId||d.characterId===context.characterId)){try{window.__SHEET_TEST_API__?.setState?.(d.sheetState||d.state||{});sendState();post('TABLEGATE_IMPORT_APPLIED',{systemId:context.sheetId})}catch(err){post('TABLEGATE_IMPORT_ERROR',{message:err.message})}}});addEventListener('load',()=>{installImporter();post('TABLEGATE_SHEET_READY',{title:document.title,systemId:context.sheetId})});if(document.readyState!=='loading')installImporter();else document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',installImporter);
})();
